(63)

where

\displaystyle\mathchoice{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\make x^{\alpha}\delta x^{\nu}.

This is the full geodesic deviation action written up to the quadratic order in geodesic deviation variables,
\nabla_{\vec{l}}\,\delta\vec{x}
and
\delta\vec{x}
, in order to study the first order part of the dynamics of a collection of null geodesics. We now write its equivalent form by making use of the relation between the covariant variation of
\vec{l}
and the derivative of the geodesic deviation vector, i.e., the relations given in eq. (
61
). Then, we have

\displaystyle S=\int\left(\frac{1}{2}\langle\,\Delta\vec{l},\Delta\vec{l}\,\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}{R^{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.5382pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.71596pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.09373pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}_{{\delta\vec{x}}{\vec{l}}{\,\vec{l}}{\delta\vec{x}}}}\right)d\lambda.

(64)

Once we treat the integrand of the action given above as the Lagrangian and
\lambda
as the evolution parameter, we can switch to a Hamiltonian formalism by making use of the parallel propagated tetrad components of
\Delta\vec{l}
and
\delta\vec{x}
.
Particularly, once we choose
\delta{x}^{\tilde{a}}
to be the canonical coordinates and
\Delta{l}_{\tilde{b}}
to be their associated canonical momenta, the Hamiltonian of the system take the form of

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathchoice{g^{{\tilde{a}}{\tilde{b}}}_{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}{g^{{\tilde{a}}{\tilde{b}}}_{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}{g^{{\tilde{a}}{\tilde{b}}}_{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}{g^{{\tilde{a}}{\tilde{b}}}_{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\Delta{l}_{\tilde{a}}\Delta{l}_{\tilde{b}}-\mathchoice{\mathcal{R}^{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.72223pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.72223pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.55557pt][c]{$\scriptscriptsty
